# Team → Nexo: independent source inventory

**Coverage: all requested top-level areas inspected; source-only, with unresolved details explicitly marked UNSURE.** This is a broad control inventory, not a claim of exhaustive runtime parity. Not exhaustively covered: repeated help/tooltips and every dynamically generated field inside nested analysis/statistics dialogs; exact on-screen scientific captions for each plot; browser accessibility/focus behavior; deployment-only sensitivity-study availability. The missing chart-audit plan and unavailable R/live API prevent resolving the remaining dynamic-schema/download questions. No pilot or patient data were opened or used.

Baseline `c49bd0f70e3597f0d44ea7b4f97a966e122c2c4c`; task branch `router/20261106-212605-60d8bd`; 2026-11-06. Source audit only: no R, browser, live API, tests or data run. No other independent reviewer’s report read. Only this report is changed.

**Commit blocked:** Git cannot create the linked worktree’s index.lock outside the writable sandbox. No commit exists for this report yet.

The requested `docs/integration/nexo-chart-audit-plan.md` is absent (`cat` → No such file or directory). Phase labels follow the request: 1 canvas (including analysis controls used to remake a figure), 2 preparation/Data/Review, 3 login/accounts, OUT Companion AI and review/foreground flow. OUT is documented in `docs/integration/nexo-team-gap-audit.md:6–9`; other intentional differences remain inventoried as capability differences. Statuses are source-level capability judgments, not runtime certification. UNSURE states the remaining check. P1 blocks a figure workflow, P2 slows/misleads, P3 cosmetic. Counts sum manually reviewed inventory rows, not grep hits or all possible Team controls. Catalog rows count Add capability only; generic controls do not certify chart rendering. Plot-editor parity applies to supported editable Plotly views, not raster charts. Shared/static R markup is evidence only when traced through the API schema and native control renderer. Dynamic choices, conditional controls and individual module download discovery remain UNSURE where a live response is needed. One repeated control per analysis context is a separate row; effort for common fixes is not additive.

## Reading the findings

The main phase-1 blockers are shared differential/filter/cohort settings, action buttons lost between the API schema and React form, custom ordered/compound controls, and direct vector-PDF export. Ordinary static fields and the 58-entry Add catalog do not establish complete workflow parity.

- **Shared settings:** API sessions open the checkpoint's preparation (`R/frontends/api/session.R:230–240`); per-card settings are local. Reopening a prepared checkpoint is supported, but changing its scientific setup is a separate capability.
- **Actions:** `R/frontends/api/schema.R:124–137` returns actions separately, while `frontends/nexo/src/ui/forms/SchemaForm.tsx:55` renders only controls (N25). This affects several analysis-generation buttons even though an API action endpoint exists.
- **Custom controls:** the literal input allow-list excludes computed IDs and sortable widgets (`R/frontends/team/plot_instances.R:105–110,139`); the renderer flattens ordered multi-selects and scalarizes ranges (`frontends/nexo/src/ui/forms/SchemaForm.tsx:40,53`).
- **Exports:** distinguish selected live plots, finished snapshots, Figure page, module downloads and Export Studio. A PDF in one path does not replace a vector PDF of an edited plot in another.
- **Uncertainty:** UNSURE is not a gap count. In particular, each module download needs its live discovery response, and some dynamically populated selectors need a live schema. An R-enabled follow-up should check those rows first.

P1 means the missing capability blocks **a workflow that needs it**, not that every figure is impossible. Repeated buttons and fields are counted by context; fixing a shared mechanism can close many rows at once.

The earlier audit also omits shared differential contrasts, global lipid filters/targeted selection, analysis tables, reusable palette import/export, most Data/Review/account controls, and the 3D viewer. These are additional scope findings, not retroactive changes to its original denominator. The milestone log’s “whole canvas inventory” claim (`docs/integration/nexo-phase1.md:493`, heading/context) should therefore be read as catalog/fixture coverage, not proof that every production control is reachable in Nexo. No execution evidence in that log was treated as an independently rerun test.
Additional export guard finding: the broad finished-download parity claim at :235 also misses stale-snapshot refusal and in-flight revision checks. Compare `www/team/js/canvas.js:2358–2359` / `www/team/js/plot_exports.js:50–52` with `frontends/nexo/src/ui/Inspector/ExportTab.tsx:29–30` / `frontends/nexo/src/ui/Dialogs/ExportDialog.tsx:79` (N67).
